To create a VS Code extension for extracting links from the `package.json` file, you can use the following steps:

1. **Install VS Code Extension Development Tools**:
   - Open VS Code.
   - Go to the Extensions view (Ctrl+Shift+X).
   - Search for "JavaScript/TypeScript" and install the "JavaScript/TypeScript: Create Extension" extension.

2. **Create a New Extension Project**:
   - In the Extensions view, click on the gear icon in the top-right corner.
   - Select "Open Folder with Default Layout".
   - Navigate to the directory where you want to create your new extension project and open it.

3. **Install Dependencies**:
   - Run the following command in the terminal:
     ```sh
     npm install
     ```

4. **Create the Extension Package JSON**:
   - Open the `package.json` file located at the root of your extension project.
   - Modify the `name`, `version`, and other properties as needed.

5. **Add a Command for Link Extraction**:
   - In the `src/commands/link-extraction.js` file, add the following code:
     ```javascript
     import { window } from 'vscode';
     import * as fs from 'fs';

     export default function extractLinks() {
       const packageJsonPath = `${workspace.rootPath}/package.json`;
       if (!fs.existsSync(packageJsonPath)) {
         return void window.showErrorMessage('No package.json found in the workspace.');
       }

       const content = fs.readFileSync(packageJsonPath, 'utf8');
       const json = JSON.parse(content);

       const links = [];
       for (const key in json) {
         if (key === 'dependencies' || key === 'devDependencies') {
           const dependencies = json[key];
           Object.keys(dependencies).forEach(dep => {
             const value = dependencies[dep];
             if (typeof value === 'string' && !value.startsWith('"')) {
               links.push(value);
             }
           });
         }
       }

       return void window.showInformationMessage(`Extracted Links: ${links.join('\n')}`);
     }
     ```

6. **Create a Command to Run the Link Extraction**:
   - In the `src/commands/link-extraction.ts` file, add the following code:
     ```typescript
     import * as vscode from 'vscode';
     import { extractLinks } from './link-extraction';

     export const activate = function (context: vscode.ExtensionContext) {
       context.subscriptions.push(
         vscode.commands.registerCommand('extension.linkExtraction', extractLinks)
       );
     };

     export function deactivate() {}
     ```

7. **Create the Command Menu**:
   - In the `src/commands/link-extraction.ts` file, add the following code:
     ```typescript
     import * as vscode from 'vscode';
     import { activate } from './link-extraction';

     export const activate = function (context: vscode.ExtensionContext) {
       context.subscriptions.push(
         vscode.commands.registerCommand('extension.linkExtraction', extractLinks)
       );
     };

     export function deactivate() {}
     ```

8. **Create the Extension Manifest**:
   - In the `src/manifest.json` file, add the following code:
     ```json
     {
       "name": "Link Extraction",
       "version": "1.0.0",
       "description": "Extracts dependencies and devDependencies from package.json",
       "main": "dist/out/main.js",
       "contributes": {
         "commands": [
           {
             "command": "extension.linkExtraction",
             "title": "Extract Links from package.json"
           }
         ]
       },
       "activationEvents": ["onCommand:extension.linkExtraction"]
     }
     ```

9. **Build and Run the Extension**:
   - Open the terminal in VS Code.
   - Run the following command to build the extension:
     ```sh
     npm run build
     ```
   - Run the following command to start the development server:
     ```sh
     npm start
     ```

10. **Test the Command**:
    - In the VS Code terminal, run the following command:
      ```sh
      code --extensionDevelopmentPath <path-to-your-extension-directory>
      ```
    - Once the extension is activated, you can find it in the command palette (`Ctrl+Shift+P`).
    - Run the `Extract Links from package.json` command to see the extracted links.

This will create a VS Code extension that extracts dependencies and devDependencies from the `package.json` file.
